**what currently exists?** 
A highly modularized React + FastAPI + MongoDB enterprise template. Strict UI design rules () are enforced across forms and datatables.  has been heavily refactored down from ~4800 lines to ~2200 lines by extracting domain routers (Users, Roles, Offices, Levels, Audit, Import, Clients, Email/Auth). The application includes an idempotent DB Backup/Restore system, GridFS for branding assets, a robust seeder using  (which contains base64 branding assets, roles, levels, and a Super Admin), and self-service Account/Settings pages for users.

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason

                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:

        else:
            reason = self.reason

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string

        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""

        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string

            # encodings. (See PR #3538)

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)
E           requests.exceptions.HTTPError: 401 Client Error: Unauthorized for url: https://design-system-nexus-1.preview.emergentagent.com/api/auth/login

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:

        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string

            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:

        else:
            reason = self.reason

            )

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)

self = <Response [401]>

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        if http_error_msg:
>           raise HTTPError(http_error_msg, response=self)

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        """Raises :class:`HTTPError`, if one occurred."""
    
        http_error_msg = ""
        if isinstance(self.reason, bytes):
            # We attempt to decode utf-8 first because some servers
            # choose to localize their reason strings. If the string
            # isn't utf-8, we fall back to iso-8859-1 for all other
            # encodings. (See PR #3538)
            try:
                reason = self.reason.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                reason = self.reason.decode("iso-8859-1")
        else:
            reason = self.reason
    
        if 400 <= self.status_code < 500:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Client Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
    
        elif 500 <= self.status_code < 600:
            http_error_msg = (
                f"{self.status_code} Server Error: {reason} for url: {self.url}"
            )
